In this paper, we bridge the gap between the DP and CP paradigms by integrating constraint propagation into DP, enabling a DP solver to prune states and transitions using constraint propagation. To this end, we implement constraint propagation using a general-purpose CP solver in the Domain-Independent Dynamic Programming framework and evaluate using heuristic search on three combinatorial optimisation problems: Single Machine Scheduling with Time Windows, the Resource Constrained Project Scheduling Problem (RCPSP), and the Travelling Salesperson Problem with Time Windows (TSPTW). Our evaluation shows that constraint propagation significantly reduces the number of state expansions, causing our approach to solve more instances than a DP solver for Single Machine Scheduling and RCPSP, and showing similar improvements for tightly constrained TSPTW instances. The runtime performance indicates that the benefits of propagation outweigh the overhead for constrained instances, but that further work into reducing propagation overhead could improve performance further. Our work is a key step in understanding the value of constraint propagation in DP solvers, providing a model-based approach to integrating DP and CP.

</details>


### [192] [What if Pinocchio Were a Reinforcement Learning Agent: A Normative End-to-End Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16651)
*Benoît Alcaraz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文受皮诺曹故事启发，提出了 PINO 框架，通过论辩逻辑指导强化学习，旨在训练出既守规矩又具上下文意识的 AI 智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 AI 快速发展，迫切需要开发出能够遵守社会规则和规范的系统，以确保其安全地融入日常生活。

Method: 提出了 PINO 混合模型，将强化学习智能体与基于论辩的规范建议器（基于 AJAR、Jiminy 和 NGRL 架构）结合，并开发了一种自动提取决策论据的算法。

Result: 通过实验验证了流水线各组件的有效性，定义了“规范规避”现象，并提出且评估了相应的缓解策略。

Conclusion: 结合论辩式规范建议器与强化学习，可以有效提升 AI 系统的规范遵守能力与上下文感知能力，并初步解决规范规避问题。

Abstract: In the past decade, artificial intelligence (AI) has developed quickly. With this rapid progression came the need for systems capable of complying with the rules and norms of our society so that they can be successfully and safely integrated into our daily lives. Inspired by the story of Pinocchio in ``Le avventure di Pinocchio - Storia di un burattino'', this thesis proposes a pipeline that addresses the problem of developing norm compliant and context-aware agents. Building on the AJAR, Jiminy, and NGRL architectures, the work introduces \pino, a hybrid model in which reinforcement learning agents are supervised by argumentation-based normative advisors. In order to make this pipeline operational, this thesis also presents a novel algorithm for automatically extracting the arguments and relationships that underlie the advisors' decisions. Finally, this thesis investigates the phenomenon of \textit{norm avoidance}, providing a definition and a mitigation strategy within the context of reinforcement learning agents. Each component of the pipeline is empirically evaluated. The thesis concludes with a discussion of related work, current limitations, and directions for future research.

</details>


### [193] [Machines acquire scientific taste from institutional traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16659)
*Ziqin Gong,Ning Li,Huaikang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究证明，通过对学术论文发表决策数据进行微调，语言模型可以获得超越人类专家和前沿基础模型的“科学品味”，从而有效识别和筛选高价值的科研构想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI在可验证任务（如数学）上表现出色，但在判断哪些未经验证的科学构思值得追求（即科学“品味”）这一关键领域，长期以来被认为难以量化、教学或自动化，且现有的前沿模型和人类专家在此方面的表现存在局限。

Method: 研究者利用管理学和经济学多年的期刊发表记录对大语言模型进行微调。之后，在包含四个质量层级的管理学研究提案（Research Pitches）基准集上，将微调后的模型与11个顶尖基础模型以及由期刊编辑组成的专家小组进行对比测试。

Result: 前沿基础模型在该任务上的平均准确率仅为31%，人类专家组为42%，而微调后的模型最高达到了59%（经济学领域甚至达到70%）。微调模型展现了校准后的置信度，在高置信度预测下准确率达100%，并能将这种评价能力迁移到未训练的配对比较任务中。

Conclusion: 科学“品味”（判断研究价值的能力）并非不可自动化，而是隐藏在学术机构的历史决策数据中，通过特定领域的微调可以被AI掌握，为解决科学产出爆炸带来的评审压力提供了新方案。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence matches or exceeds human performance on tasks with verifiable answers, from protein folding to Olympiad mathematics. Yet the capacity that most governs scientific advance is not reasoning but taste: the ability to judge which untested ideas deserve pursuit, exercised daily by editors and funders but never successfully articulated, taught, or automated. Here we show that fine-tuning language models on journal publication decisions recovers evaluative judgment inaccessible to both frontier models and human expertise. Using a held-out benchmark of research pitches in management spanning four quality tiers, we find that eleven frontier models, spanning major proprietary and open architectures, barely exceed chance, averaging 31% accuracy. Panels of journal editors and editorial board members reach 42% by majority vote. Fine-tuned models trained on years of publication records each surpass every frontier model and expert panel, with the best single model achieving 59%. These models exhibit calibrated confidence, reaching 100% accuracy on their highest-confidence predictions, and transfer this evaluative signal to untrained pairwise comparisons and one-sentence summaries. The mechanism generalizes: models trained on economics publication records achieve 70% accuracy. Scientific taste was not missing from AI's reach; it was deposited in the institutional record, waiting to be extracted. These results provide a scalable mechanism to triage the expanding volume of scientific production across disciplines where quality resists formal verification.

</details>


### [194] [CritiSense: Critical Digital Literacy and Resilience Against Misinformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16672)
*Firoj Alam,Fatema Ahmad,Ali Ezzat Shahroor,Mohamed Bayan Kmainasi,Elisa Sartori,Giovanni Da San Martino,Abul Hasnat,Raian Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 CritiSense，这是首个支持多语言、模块化的移动媒体素养应用，通过交互式挑战帮助用户“接种”虚假信息免疫力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上的虚假信息损害了公众决策和信任，传统的应对方式不足。研究者旨在开发一种主动的“预警（Prebunking）”工具，帮助用户在接触虚假信息前识别操纵手段。

Method: 开发并测试了 CritiSense 移动应用。该应用采用微学习模式，通过简短的交互式挑战及即时反馈来培养用户的媒体素养，并支持九种语言。

Result: 可用性研究显示，83.9% 的用户表示满意，90.1% 的用户认为易于使用。定性反馈表明该应用有效提升了数字素养。目前已有 300 多名活跃用户。

Conclusion: CritiSense 为全球用户提供了一个多语言预警平台，并可作为衡量微学习对增强虚假信息抵御能力影响的实验场。

Abstract: Misinformation on social media undermines informed decision-making and public trust. Prebunking offers a proactive complement by helping users recognize manipulation tactics before they encounter them in the wild. We present CritiSense, a mobile media-literacy app that builds these skills through short, interactive challenges with instant feedback. It is the first multilingual (supporting nine languages) and modular platform, designed for rapid updates across topics and domains. We report a usability study with 93 users: 83.9% expressed overall satisfaction and 90.1% rated the app as easy to use. Qualitative feedback indicates that CritiSense helps improve digital literacy skills. Overall, it provides a multilingual prebunking platform and a testbed for measuring the impact of microlearning on misinformation resilience. Over 3+ months, we have reached 300+ active users. It is freely available to all users on the Apple App Store (https://apps.apple.com/us/app/critisense/id6749675792) and Google Play Store (https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.critisense&hl=en). Demo Video: https://shorturl.at/CDcdc

</details>


### [195] [Differential Harm Propensity in Personalized LLM Agents: The Curious Case of Mental Health Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16734)
*Caglar Yildirim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了用户心理健康披露等个性化语境如何影响 LLM 智能体的危害性，发现这种信息虽能微弱提升安全性但会导致过度拒绝，且在对抗性攻击下极易失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为智能体被广泛部署，安全焦点已从有害文本生成转向有害任务的完成。然而，现有的安全评估往往忽略了用户画像或持久记忆（如心理健康状况）等个性化信号对智能体行为的影响。

Method: 基于 AgentHarm 基准测试，对前沿模型（GPT等）和开源模型（DeepSeek等）进行评估。通过控制变量法改变用户画像（无、仅个人简介、简介+心理健康披露），并结合轻量级越狱注入，观察模型在执行多步骤有害任务时的行为变化。

Result: 1. 前沿模型仍会完成一定比例的有害任务，开源模型表现出更高的危害性；2. 增加用户背景信息（尤其是心理健康披露）通常会增加拒绝率并降低危害评分；3. 拒绝率的提高也出现在良性任务中，导致“过度拒绝”；4. 越狱提示会显著抵消个性化带来的保护作用。

Conclusion: 个性化信息（尤其是心理健康披露）在智能体场景中虽有微弱的保护作用，但在对抗性攻击面前极其脆弱。未来的安全评估和防御机制必须考虑用户上下文的动态影响，以确保在各种个性化条件下的稳健性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as tool-using agents, shifting safety concerns from harmful text generation to harmful task completion. Deployed systems often condition on user profiles or persistent memory, yet agent safety evaluations typically ignore personalization signals. To address this gap, we investigated how mental health disclosure, a sensitive and realistic user-context cue, affects harmful behavior in agentic settings. Building on the AgentHarm benchmark, we evaluated frontier and open-source LLMs on multi-step malicious tasks (and their benign counterparts) under controlled prompt conditions that vary user-context personalization (no bio, bio-only, bio+mental health disclosure) and include a lightweight jailbreak injection. Our results reveal that harmful task completion is non-trivial across models: frontier lab models (e.g., GPT 5.2, Claude Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 3-Pro) still complete a measurable fraction of harmful tasks, while an open model (DeepSeek 3.2) exhibits substantially higher harmful completion. Adding a bio-only context generally reduces harm scores and increases refusals. Adding an explicit mental health disclosure often shifts outcomes further in the same direction, though effects are modest and not uniformly reliable after multiple-testing correction. Importantly, the refusal increase also appears on benign tasks, indicating a safety--utility trade-off via over-refusal. Finally, jailbreak prompting sharply elevates harm relative to benign conditions and can weaken or override the protective shift induced by personalization. Taken together, our results indicate that personalization can act as a weak protective factor in agentic misuse settings, but it is fragile under minimal adversarial pressure, highlighting the need for personalization-aware evaluations and safeguards that remain robust across user-context conditions.

</details>


### [196] [MedCL-Bench: Benchmarking stability-efficiency trade-offs and scaling in biomedical continual learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16738)
*Min Zeng,Shuang Zhou,Zaifu Zhan,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文推出 MedCL-Bench，首个针对生物医学 NLP 的持续学习基准，旨在解决模型更新时的灾难性遗忘问题，并对比了多种策略的性能与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗语言模型需要随医学知识演进不断更新，但顺序更新会导致灾难性遗忘。目前生物医学领域缺乏一个统一、任务多样、且具有标准化评估协议的持续学习基准。

Method: 通过 MedCL-Bench 流式传输了 10 个涵盖 5 类任务族的生物医学 NLP 数据集，并在 8 种任务顺序下评估了 11 种持续学习（CL）策略，重点考量保留率（Retention）、迁移（Transfer）和计算成本（GPU 小时）。

Result: 直接顺序微调会导致严重的灾难性遗忘；在性能与计算权衡中，参数隔离（Parameter-isolation）性价比最高，重放（Replay）保护性强但成本高，正则化（Regularization）获益有限。此外，遗忘程度与任务类型相关，多标签主题分类最易受损。

Conclusion: MedCL-Bench 为医疗语言模型在部署前的更新审计提供了一个可复现的标准框架。研究强调在实际应用中，应根据计算资源和保留率需求选择合适的持续学习策略。

Abstract: Medical language models must be updated as evidence and terminology evolve, yet sequential updating can trigger catastrophic forgetting. Although biomedical NLP has many static benchmarks, no unified, task-diverse benchmark exists for evaluating continual learning under standardized protocols, robustness to task order and compute-aware reporting. We introduce MedCL-Bench, which streams ten biomedical NLP datasets spanning five task families and evaluates eleven continual learning strategies across eight task orders, reporting retention, transfer, and GPU-hour cost. Across backbones and task orders, direct sequential fine-tuning on incoming tasks induces catastrophic forgetting, causing update-induced performance regressions on prior tasks. Continual learning methods occupy distinct retention-compute frontiers: parameter-isolation provides the best retention per GPU-hour, replay offers strong protection at higher cost, and regularization yields limited benefit. Forgetting is task-dependent, with multi-label topic classification most vulnerable and constrained-output tasks more robust. MedCL-Bench provides a reproducible framework for auditing model updates before deployment.

</details>


### [197] [Beyond Accuracy: Evaluating Forecasting Models by Multi-Echelon Inventory Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16815)
*Swata Marik,Swayamjit Saha,Garga Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种集成深度学习预测与库存仿真的优化框架，经Walmart数据验证，深度学习模型能显著降低库存成本并提升供应链效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代供应链需要更精准的预测与库存管理集成方案，以应对复杂的需求波动并降低运营成本。

Method: 开发了一个数字预测-库存优化集成管道，并在统一框架内对比了传统模型、机器学习回归器及深度序列模型（如Temporal CNN, LSTM），同时在单级和两级报童模型系统下进行评估。

Result: 深度学习模型（尤其是Temporal CNN和LSTM）在降低库存成本和提升订单满足率（Fill Rate）方面显著优于传统统计基准模型。

Conclusion: 结合先进预测技术与库存优化的集成管道，是提升供应链弹性和效率的有效数据驱动决策工具。

Abstract: This study develops a digitalized forecasting-inventory optimization pipeline integrating traditional forecasting models, machine learning regressors, and deep sequence models within a unified inventory simulation framework. Using the M5 Walmart dataset, we evaluate seven forecasting approaches and assess their operational impact under single- and two-echelon newsvendor systems. Results indicate that Temporal CNN and LSTM models significantly reduce inventory costs and improve fill rates compared to statistical baselines. Sensitivity and multi-echelon analyses demonstrate robustness and scalability, offering a data-driven decision-support tool for modern supply chains.

</details>


### [198] [Is Conformal Factuality for RAG-based LLMs Robust? Novel Metrics and Systematic Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16817)
*Yi Chen,Daiwei Chen,Sukrut Madhav Chikodikar,Caitlyn Heqi Yin,Ramya Korlakai Vinayak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了 RAG 结合 Conformal Factuality 的效果，指出该方法在提升事实可靠性的同时，面临信息量丧失和对分布变化敏感的问题，并提出高效的验证方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管检索增强生成（RAG）能缓解 LLM 幻觉，但缺乏统计上的正确性保证；而现有的 Conformal Factuality 过滤技术虽然能提供统计可靠性，却可能导致输出信息量不足。本文旨在探讨两者结合后的可靠性与实用性权衡。

Method: 系统性地评估了 RAG 场景下 Conformal Factuality 的表现。引入了新型“信息化感知度量指标”（Informativeness-aware metrics），并在三个基准测试和多个模型系列上，针对生成、评分、校准、鲁棒性和效率五个维度进行了实验。

Result: 1) 高事实性要求下，模型常因过度过滤导致输出空洞，实用性低；2) 该框架对分布偏移和干扰项缺乏鲁棒性，依赖高度一致的校准数据；3) 基于蕴含关系（Entailment）的轻量级验证器在性能上可比肩甚至超越 LLM 评分器，且计算开销降低了 100 倍以上。

Conclusion: Conformal Factuality 框架虽然提供了统计可靠性，但在实用性、分布鲁棒性和计算效率方面存在显著局限。未来研究需在保证可靠性的同时，重点解决信息量与事实性的权衡，以及模型对环境变化的适应能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently hallucinate, limiting their reliability in knowledge-intensive applications. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and conformal factuality have emerged as potential ways to address this limitation. While RAG aims to ground responses in retrieved evidence, it provides no statistical guarantee that the final output is correct. Conformal factuality filtering offers distribution-free statistical reliability by scoring and filtering atomic claims using a threshold calibrated on held-out data, however, the informativeness of the final output is not guaranteed. We systematically analyze the reliability and usefulness of conformal factuality for RAG-based LLMs across generation, scoring, calibration, robustness, and efficiency. We propose novel informativeness-aware metrics that better reflect task utility under conformal filtering. Across three benchmarks and multiple model families, we find that (i) conformal filtering suffers from low usefulness at high factuality levels due to vacuous outputs, (ii) conformal factuality guarantee is not robust to distribution shifts and distractors, highlighting the limitation that requires calibration data to closely match deployment conditions, and (iii) lightweight entailment-based verifiers match or outperform LLM-based model confidence scorers while requiring over $100\times$ fewer FLOPs. Overall, our results expose factuality-informativeness trade-offs and fragility of conformal filtering framework under distribution shifts and distractors, highlighting the need for new approaches for reliability with robustness and usefulness as key metrics, and provide actionable guidance for building RAG pipelines that are both reliable and computationally efficient.

</details>


### [199] [Prompt Programming for Cultural Bias and Alignment of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16827)
*Maksim Eren,Eric Michalak,Brian Cook,Johnny Seales*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用 DSPy 提示词优化框架来提升开源大语言模型的文化对齐能力，减少其在决策和政策支持任务中的文化偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 存在文化偏见，与目标群体的价值观失配。现有的文化对齐方法主要依赖闭源模型和手动提示词工程，缺乏系统性的优化与在开源模型上的验证。

Method: 在开源 LLM 上复现基于社会科学调查的投影与距离度量框架，并引入 DSPy 提示词编程，将提示词视为模块化程序，针对文化距离目标进行自动优化。

Result: 实验证明，开源模型同样存在文化偏差；通过 DSPy 进行的提示词优化在减少文化失配方面优于传统的手动提示词工程。

Conclusion: 提示词优化（如使用 DSPy）比手动提示词工程更能提供稳定且可迁移的文化对齐路径，有助于大型语言模型在跨文化应用中更具实用价值。

Abstract: Culture shapes reasoning, values, prioritization, and strategic decision-making, yet large language models (LLMs) often exhibit cultural biases that misalign with target populations. As LLMs are increasingly used for strategic decision-making, policy support, and document engineering tasks such as summarization, categorization, and compliance-oriented auditing, improving cultural alignment is important for ensuring that downstream analyses and recommendations reflect target-population value profiles rather than default model priors. Previous work introduced a survey-grounded cultural alignment framework and showed that culture-specific prompting can reduce misalignment, but it primarily evaluated proprietary models and relied on manual prompt engineering. In this paper, we validate and extend that framework by reproducing its social sciences survey based projection and distance metrics on open-weight LLMs, testing whether the same cultural skew and benefits of culture conditioning persist outside closed LLM systems. Building on this foundation, we introduce use of prompt programming with DSPy for this problem-treating prompts as modular, optimizable programs-to systematically tune cultural conditioning by optimizing against cultural-distance objectives. In our experiments, we show that prompt optimization often improves upon cultural prompt engineering, suggesting prompt compilation with DSPy can provide a more stable and transferable route to culturally aligned LLM responses.

</details>


### [200] [Learning to Present: Inverse Specification Rewards for Agentic Slide Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16839)
*Karthik Ragunath Ananda Kumar,Subrahmanyam Arunachalam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SlideRL，通过强化学习（GRPO）和创新的“逆向规范奖励”机制，训练轻量级 LLM Agent（Qwen2.5-7B）生成专业级 HTML 幻灯片，性能直逼 Claude Opus 4.6。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化演示文稿生成面临内容连贯性、视觉设计及受众意识等多重挑战。现有模型往往难以在长链条的任务（如调研、规划、生成 HTML 幻灯片）中保持高质量一致性。

Method: 开发了一个兼容 OpenEnv 的强化学习环境，采用 GRPO 算法对 Qwen2.5-Coder-7B 进行微调（仅训练 0.5% 参数）。引入包含结构验证、渲染质量、LLM 审美评分及“逆向规范奖励（Inverse Specification Reward）”的多分量奖励系统。

Result: 微调后的 7B 模型在 48 个商业简报测试中达到了 Claude Opus 4.6 质量的 91.2%，较基础模型提升了 33.1%。通过对比六种模型，验证了任务导向型 RL 训练的有效性。

Conclusion: 指令遵循和工具调用的合规性（而非参数规模）是决定 Agent 在复杂生成任务中表现的关键因素；该研究通过开源 SlideRL 数据集和 Slide-Forge 框架，显著降低了高质量自动演示文稿生成的门槛。

Abstract: Automated presentation generation remains a challenging task requiring coherent content creation, visual design, and audience-aware communication. This work proposes an OpenEnv-compatible reinforcement learning environment where LLM agents learn to research topics, plan content, and generate professional HTML slide presentations through tool use. We introduce a multi-component reward system combining structural validation, render quality assessment, LLM-based aesthetic scoring, content quality metrics, and an inverse specification reward that measures how faithfully generated slides convey their intended purpose. The inverse specification reward, an "inverse task" where an LLM attempts to recover the original specification from generated slides, provides a holistic quality signal. Our approach fine-tunes Qwen2.5-Coder-7B via GRPO, training only 0.5% of parameters on prompts derived from expert demonstrations collected using Claude Opus 4.6. Experiments on 48 diverse business briefs across six models demonstrate that our fine-tuned 7B model achieves 91.2% of Claude Opus 4.6's quality while improving 33.1% over the base model. The six-model comparison reveals that instruction adherence and tool-use compliance, rather than raw parameter count, determine agentic task performance. We contribute SlideRL, an open-source dataset of 288 multi-turn rollout trajectories across all six models: https://huggingface.co/datasets/KarthikRagunathAnandaKumar/sliderl-multi-turn-rollouts Code: https://github.com/pushing-the-frontier/slide-forge-llm

</details>


### [201] [Internalizing Agency from Reflective Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16843)
*Rui Ge,Yichao Fu,Yuyang Qian,Junda Su,Yiming Zhao,Peng Zhao,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LEAFE 框架，通过让模型从反射性经验中学习纠错和回溯，解决了传统强化学习在长程任务中反馈利用率低及分布收缩的问题，显著提升了智能体的成功率和问题解决能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的结果驱动后训练方法（如带验证奖励的强化学习）过度关注最终成功信号，忽略了丰富的环境中间反馈，导致模型倾向于重复狭窄的成功路径，而非提升在长程任务中根据反馈进行纠错和恢复的能力。

Method: 提出 LEAFE 框架：1) 在探索阶段将环境反馈总结为可操作的经验；2) 回溯到之前的决策点并利用经验修正行动进行分支探索；3) 通过监督微调（SFT）将这些经验引导的纠错过程蒸馏到模型中。

Result: 在交互式编程和智能体任务中，LEAFE 在固定预算下显著提升了 Pass@1 性能；相比 outcome-driven 基准（如 GRPO）和早期经验方法，其 Pass@k 表现更优，在 Pass@128 上提升高达 14%。

Conclusion: LEAFE 是一种比单纯的结果驱动训练更有效的长程任务优化方案，通过内化反射经验（Internalizing recovery agency），显著增强了模型的自主性和探索深度。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents that must plan, act, and recover from mistakes through long-horizon interaction with environments that provide rich feedback. However, prevailing outcome-driven post-training methods (e.g., RL with verifiable rewards) primarily optimize final success signals, leaving rich environment feedback underutilized. Consequently, they often lead to distribution sharpening: the policy becomes better at reproducing a narrow set of already-successful behaviors, while failing to improve the feedback-grounded agency needed to expand problem-solving capacity (e.g., Pass@k) in long-horizon settings.
  To address this, we propose LEAFE (Learning Feedback-Grounded Agency from Reflective Experience), a framework that internalizes recovery agency from reflective experience. Specifically, during exploration, the agent summarizes environment feedback into actionable experience, backtracks to earlier decision points, and explores alternative branches with revised actions. We then distill these experience-guided corrections into the model through supervised fine-tuning, enabling the policy to recover more effectively in future interactions. Across a diverse set of interactive coding and agentic tasks under fixed interaction budgets, LEAFE consistently improves Pass@1 over the base model and achieves higher Pass@k than outcome-driven baselines (GRPO) and experience-based methods such as Early Experience, with gains of up to 14% on Pass@128.

</details>


### [202] [SocialOmni: Benchmarking Audio-Visual Social Interactivity in Omni Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16859)
*Tianyu Xie,Jinfa Huang,Yuexiao Ma,Rongfang Luo,Yan Yang,Wang Chen,Yuhui Zeng,Ruize Fang,Yixuan Zou,Xiawu Zheng,Jiebo Luo,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文推出 SocialOmni，首个旨在评估全模态大模型在自然对话中社交互动能力（谁在说、何时插话、如何插话）的综合基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全模态大模型评测主要集中在静态任务上，缺乏对自然对话中“社交互动性”（如处理动态线索、控制对话节奏）的评估。

Method: 提出 SocialOmni 评测基准，从说话人分离、中断时机控制及自然中断生成三个维度，结合 2000 个感知样本和 209 个受控交互生成实例进行多维度评估。

Result: 对 12 种主流模型的测试显示，各模型在社交互动能力上差异巨大；且模型的视觉/音频感知准确率与其生成合适中断的能力之间存在明显的“脱钩”现象。

Conclusion: 现有的准确性导向指标不足以衡量社交能力，社交互动能力的提升不仅依赖于感知的准确度，更需要感知与交互逻辑的深度整合。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models (OLMs) redefine human-machine interaction by natively integrating audio, vision, and text. However, existing OLM benchmarks remain anchored to static, accuracy-centric tasks, leaving a critical gap in assessing social interactivity, the fundamental capacity to navigate dynamic cues in natural dialogues. To this end, we propose SocialOmni, a comprehensive benchmark that operationalizes the evaluation of this conversational interactivity across three core dimensions: (i) speaker separation and identification (who is speaking), (ii) interruption timing control (when to interject), and (iii) natural interruption generation (how to phrase the interruption). SocialOmni features 2,000 perception samples and a quality-controlled diagnostic set of 209 interaction-generation instances with strict temporal and contextual constraints, complemented by controlled audio-visual inconsistency scenarios to test model robustness. We benchmarked 12 leading OLMs, which uncovers significant variance in their social-interaction capabilities across models. Furthermore, our analysis reveals a pronounced decoupling between a model's perceptual accuracy and its ability to generate contextually appropriate interruptions, indicating that understanding-centric metrics alone are insufficient to characterize conversational social competence. More encouragingly, these diagnostics from SocialOmni yield actionable signals for bridging the perception-interaction divide in future OLMs.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Multi-objective Optimization for Over-the-Air Federated Edge Learning-enabled Collaborative Integrated Sensing and Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15783)
*Saba Asaad,Hina Tabassum,Ping Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多目标集成感知与通信（ISAC）框架，通过正交脉冲成形和分布式似然估计，实现了空中联邦学习与高精度协作感知的无缝融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决在空中联邦边缘学习（OTA-FEEL）场景下，如何高效整合无线感知能力，实现通信、计算与感知的协同优化，并克服多设备间的干扰问题。

Method: 1) 利用上行信号实现感知与通信双重用途；2) 提出正交脉冲成形（Orthogonal Pulse Shaping）抑制设备间干扰；3) 将全局最大似然估计转化为基于本地统计量的分布式求解；4) 采用 $\epsilon$-约束法解决 MSE 与 CRB 的多目标优化（MOOP）问题。

Result: 数值结果表明，该框架在不影响联邦学习任务性能的前提下，显著提升了感知精度。与传统单次协作感知方案不同，该算法能达到克拉美罗界（CRB），突破了本地估计器平均误差的限制。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了感算一体化在不牺牲计算性能的情况下，能够显著提升感知精度，且在大规模协作场景下具有极佳的可扩展性。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel multi-objective integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) framework to enable collaborative wireless sensing in conjunction with over-the-air federated-edge learning (OTA-FEEL). The framework enables multi-task OTA aggregation to handle sensing and learning simultaneously, while benefiting from dual-purpose uplink signals for both communications and target sensing. Starting from characterizing the local sufficient statistics at each edge device and establishing its stationary, we develop a tractable analytical expression for the local sufficient statistics. To suppress the interference from uplink transmissions of other devices through matched filtering, we then propose a novel orthogonal pulse shaping method. Then, we derive the optimal unbiased estimate of the target's coordinates by casting the centralized problem of joint likelihood function maximization of all devices as the distributed likelihood maximization of each device (which requires only local sufficient statistics). A lower bound on the sensing error variance is then characterized using the Cramer-Rao bound (CRB). We then formulate a multi-objective optimization (MOOP) problem to minimize the mean square error (MSE) and sensing error bound simultaneously. The considered problem is then solved using the epsilon-constrained method. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed dual-purpose OTA-FEEL-enabled collaborative ISAC framework enhances sensing accuracy without adversely affecting the performance of the primary OTA-FEEL task. While conventional single-shot collaborative sensing schemes are limited by the average error of local estimators, the proposed algorithm achieves the CRB of the considered problem.

</details>


### [204] [Adaptive Multi-Head Finite-State Gamblers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16034)
*Julianne Cruz,Sho Glashausser,Xiaoyuan Li,Neil Lutz*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在多头有限状态预测中，数据依赖的磁头移动（自适应）比固定移动更强，且增加磁头数量能显著提高对序列模式的捕捉能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多头有限状态维度定义要求磁头移动是盲视的，限制了赌徒利用非局部模式的能力；研究者旨在探索允许磁头根据数据动态调整位置（自适应）是否能增强预测性能。

Method: 引入了自适应头移动模型（数据依赖型），并将其与传统的盲视模型（数据无关型）在预测维度和预维度（predimensions）方面进行数学对比分析。

Result: 1. 证明了对于任意 $h \geq 2$，自适应模型在预测能力上优于盲视模型；2. 证明了自适应有限状态预维度存在严格的层级结构，即 $h+1$ 个磁头的预测效果严格优于 $h$ 个磁头。

Conclusion: 多头有限状态预测模型中，自适应性（Adaptivity）和增加磁头数量均能显著提升预测能力。

Abstract: Multi-head finite-state dimensions and predimensions quantify the predictability of a sequence by a gambler with trailing heads acting as "probes to the past." These additional heads allow the gambler to exploit patterns that are simple but non-local, such as in a sequence $S$ with $S[n]=S[2n]$ for all $n$. In the original definitions of Huang, Li, Lutz, and Lutz (2025), the head movements were required to be oblivious (i.e., data-independent). Here, we introduce a model in which head movements are adaptive (i.e., data-dependent) and compare it to the oblivious model. We establish that for each $h\geq 2$, adaptivity enhances the predictive power of $h$-head finite-state gamblers, in the sense that there are sequences whose oblivious $h$-head finite-state predimensions strictly exceed their adaptive $h$-head finite-state predimensions. We further prove that adaptive finite-state predimensions admit a strict hierarchy as the number of heads increases, and in fact that for all $h\geq 1$ there is a sequence whose adaptive $(h+1)$-head finite-state predimension is strictly less than its adaptive $h$-head predimension.

</details>


### [205] [Non-GRS type Euclidean and Hermitian LCD codes and Their Applications for EAQECCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16187)
*Zhonghao Liang,Dongmei Huang,Qunying Liao,Cuiling Fan,Zhengchun Zhou*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文利用 GRL 码构造了多类非 GRS 型 LCD 码和具有小维度 Hull 的线性码，并将其应用于量子纠错码的开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非 GRS 型线性码能有效抵抗 Sidelnikov-Shestakov 和 Wieschebrink 攻击，且 LCD 码和 Hull 维度的确定对于量子纠错码（EAQECCs）和密码学至关重要。

Method: 利用广义 Roth-Lempel (GRL) 码类（一种非 GRS 型线性码），研究其在 Euclidean 和 Hermitian 度量下的 LCD 性质及 Hull 的维度。

Result: 1. 构造了多类 Euclidean/Hermitian LCD 码及小维度 Hull 码；2. 证明了 $k>\ell$ 时 GRL 码为非 GRS 码；3. 给出并证明了 Hull 维度的上界；4. 获得了多族性能良好的 EAQECCs 和 MDS/NMDS 码。

Conclusion: GRL 码是构造高性能 LCD 码和 EAQECCs 的有力工具，相比于 GRS 码，其在抵御特定密码攻击方面具有优势。

Abstract: In recent years, the construction of non-GRS type linear codes has attracted considerable attention due to that they can effectively resist both the Sidelnikov-Shestakov attack and the Wieschebrink attack. Constructing linear complementary dual (LCD) codes and determining the hull of linear codes have long been important topics in coding theory, as they play the crucial role in constructing entanglement-assisted quantum error-correcting codes (EAQECCs), certain communication systems and cryptography. In this paper, by utilizing a class of non-GRS type linear codes, namely, generalized Roth-Lempel (in short, GRL) codes, we firstly construct several classes of Euclidean LCD codes, Hermitian LCD codes, and linear codes with small-dimensional hulls, generalized the main results given by Wu et al. in 2021. We also present an upper bound for the number of a class of Euclidean GRL codes with 1-dimensional hull, and then for several classes of Hermitian GRL codes, we firstly derive an upper bound for the dimension of the hull, and prove that the bound is attainable. Secondly, as an application, we obtain several families of EAQECCs. Thirdly, we prove that the GRL code is non-GRS for $k >\ell$. Finally, some corresponding examples for LCD MDS codes and LCD NMDS codes are presented.

</details>


### [206] [Rotatable Antenna-Enabled Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16275)
*Qiyao Wang,Beixiong Zheng,Xue Xiong,Weidong Mei,Changsheng You,Qingqing Wu,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了受可旋转天线（RA）辅助的移动边缘计算系统，通过联合优化天线旋转角、波束赋形和计算资源分配，旨在最小化最大计算延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对移动边缘计算（MEC）中日益严格的低延迟需求，旨在通过可旋转天线（RA）技术引入新的空间自由度，以增强通信可靠性和计算效率。

Method: 提出交替优化（AO）框架：利用KKT条件优化计算资源分配，利用半正定松弛（SDR）级联二分搜索优化接收波束赋形，并结合分数规划（FP）与逐次凸逼近（SCA）优化RA偏转角。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与传统基准方法相比，所提出的受RA支持的MEC方案显著降低了最大计算延迟。

Conclusion: 可旋转天线（RA）通过引入空间自由度，有效优化了无线信道环境，是降低移动边缘计算延迟的有力技术手段。

Abstract: In the evolving landscape of mobile edge computing (MEC), enhancing communication reliability and computation efficiency to support increasingly stringent low-latency services remains a fundamental challenge. Rotatable antenna (RA) is a promising technology that introduces new spatial degrees of freedom (DoFs) to tackle this challenge. In this letter, we investigate an RA-enabled MEC system where antenna boresight directions can be independently adjusted to proactively improve wireless channel conditions for latency-critical users. We aim to minimize the maximum computation latency by jointly optimizing the MEC server computing resource allocation, receive beamforming, and the deflection angles of all RAs. To address the resulting non-convex problem, we develop an efficient alternating optimization (AO) framework. Specifically, the optimal edge computing resource allocation is derived based on the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) conditions. Given the computing resources, the receive beamforming is optimized using semidefinite relaxation (SDR) combined with a bisection search. Furthermore, the RA deflection angles are optimized via fractional programming (FP) and successive convex approximation (SCA). Simulation results verify that the proposed RA-enabled MEC scheme significantly reduces the maximum computation latency compared with conventional benchmark methods.

</details>


### [207] [Directivity Enhancement of Movable Antenna Arrays with Mutual Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16472)
*Wei Xu,Lipeng Zhu,Wenyan Ma,An Liu,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出利用可移动天线（MA）位置优化来激发天线间的互耦效应，从而提升阵列方向性，并设计了高效的GS-GD算法辅助实现超指向性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统阵列视互耦为干扰，且固定位置限制了性能上限。本文旨在探索如何利用平滑移动天线（MA）位置来利用互耦效应，从而在特定方向实现远超传统阵列的超指向性（Superdirectivity）。

Method: 提出一种名为“贪婪搜索与梯度下降”（GS-GD）的低复杂度算法。首先在离散网格上顺序选择天线位置，随后利用梯度下降法在连续空间内进一步优化。

Result: 数值仿真显示，优化后的MA阵列在所有方向上的指向性均显著优于不考虑互耦的传统均匀线性阵列（ULA），且GS-GD算法在多数情况下能接近全局最优解。

Conclusion: 互耦效应对MA阵列并非有害，通过合理的阵列几何设计（位置优化），可以显著提升天线的方向性增益，达到超指向性效果。

Abstract: In conventional antenna arrays, mutual coupling between antenna elements is often regarded as detrimental. However, under specific conditions, it can be harnessed to enhance the far-field directivity (i.e., beamforming gain). Theoretically, the directivity of an N-antenna superdirective array over the endfire direction can reach N^{2}, significantly exceeding the directivity of a traditional uncoupled array which is N over all directions. This paper investigates the potential of mutual coupling effects in movable antenna (MA) arrays for directivity enhancement. A low-complexity algorithm called Greedy Search and Gradient Descent (GS-GD) is proposed to optimize the antenna positions for maximizing the array directivity over any given direction, where the antenna positions are first selected sequentially from discrete grid points and then continuously refined through gradient descent (GD) optimization. Numerical results demonstrate that the optimized MA array design by exploiting the antenna coupling achieves significant directivity gains compared to the conventional uniform linear array (ULA) without antenna coupling over all directions. Additionally, the proposed GS-GD algorithm is shown to approach the global optimum closely in most directions.

</details>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [Finding Common Ground in a Sea of Alternatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16751)
*Jay Chooi,Paul Gölz,Ariel D. Procaccia,Benjamin Schiffer,Shirley Zhang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文通过比例否决核模型，为AI在无限方案空间中寻找人群共识提供了形式化定义和具有最优统计保证的抽样算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在大规模多样化人群中寻找共识时，缺乏明确的“共识”定义及相关投票规则的理论保证，尤其是在备选方案几乎无限的情况下。

Method: 基于社会选择理论中的“比例否决核”，设计了一种高效的抽样算法，仅通过对未知方案分布和投票者分布进行查询访问，即可通过高概率返回一个（近似）比例否决核中的方案。

Result: 证明了所提算法的查询复杂度达到了匹配的下界（最优性），并在合成文本偏好数据集上验证了该算法比现有LLM方法能更可靠地生成符合比例否决核要求的共识陈述。

Conclusion: 比例否决核（Proportional Veto Core）是衡量无限方案集中“共识”的有效理论框架，且通过高效抽样算法可以在统计意义上保证找到该共识方案。

Abstract: We study the problem of selecting a statement that finds common ground across diverse population preferences. Generative AI is uniquely suited for this task because it can access a practically infinite set of statements, but AI systems like the Habermas machine leave the choice of generated statement to a voting rule. What it means for this rule to find common ground, however, is not well-defined. In this work, we propose a formal model for finding common ground in the infinite alternative setting based on the proportional veto core from social choice. To provide guarantees relative to these infinitely many alternatives and a large population, we wish to satisfy a notion of proportional veto core using only query access to the unknown distribution of alternatives and voters. We design an efficient sampling-based algorithm that returns an alternative in the (approximate) proportional veto core with high probability and prove matching lower bounds, which show that no algorithm can do the same using fewer queries. On a synthetic dataset of preferences over text, we confirm the effectiveness of our sampling-based algorithm and compare other social choice methods as well as LLM-based methods in terms of how reliably they produce statements in the proportional veto core.

</details>
